{"step_index":65,"source":"MODEL","type":"PLANNER_RESPONSE","status":"DONE","created_at":"2026-06-24T15:56:47Z","content":"I've identified and fixed the issue! \n\n**What was happening:**\nOn Android, Expo applications natively handle resizing the window when the keyboard opens (via `adjustResize`). However, the `AITab` was also wrapping the input inside a `KeyboardAvoidingView` with `behavior=\"height\"`. This caused a known conflict where Android would shrink the window, and React Native would *also* try to shrink the view. When you closed the keyboard, the view would get \"stuck\" permanently shrunk, leaving your input bar floating high up in the air (as seen in your screenshot) instead of returning to its default position above the tab bar.\n\n**How I fixed it:**\nI updated the `KeyboardAvoidingView` behavior inside `src/app/(app)/dashboard/index.tsx` so that it uses the correct padding behavior on iOS, but falls back to `undefined` on Android.
